# ============================================================================
# CHAOS REDUX - EVENT 005 CUSTOM SPLINTER FOCUS TREES
# ============================================================================
# Runtime focus packages for custom high-chaos Soviet Collapse successors.
# These tags are created during failed containment routes and use shared
# dynamic support effects plus tag-specific endgame hooks. The layout uses
# wide branch lanes with compressed depth so crisis choices do not stack into
# narrow vertical columns, while continuous focus panels sit to the right.
# ============================================================================

focus_tree = {
	id = FTH_soviet_collapse_focus_tree

	country = {

			base = 12
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 has_stability < constant:soviet_collapse_soviet_objective.good_stability }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 SOV = { check_variable = { var = soviet_collapse_total_collapse_threat value = constant:soviet_collapse_republic_focus.ai_pressure_high compare = greater_than } } }
		}
	}

			army_experience = constant:soviet_collapse_republic_focus.army_xp_small
			add_command_power = constant:soviet_collapse_republic_focus.command_power_small
			add_manpower = constant:soviet_collapse_republic_focus.militia_manpower
			add_to_variable = { dsc_roll_call_legitimacy = constant:soviet_collapse_factory_ancient.medium_mandate_gain }
			add_to_variable = { dsc_revenant_mobilization = constant:soviet_collapse_factory_ancient.small_mandate_gain }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = { is_owned_and_controlled_by = ROOT }
				construct_building_in_random_province = { bunker = 1 }
				add_building_construction = { type = infrastructure level = 1 instant_build = yes }
			}
			hidden_effect = {
				if = {
					limit = { SOV = { exists = yes } }
					add_ai_strategy = { type = prepare_for_war id = SOV value = 75 }
					add_ai_strategy = { type = antagonize id = SOV value = 50 }
				}
			}
		}

			base = 6
			modifier = { factor = 2 has_country_flag = dsc_focus_witness_officers }
			modifier = { factor = 1.5 NOT = { has_war = yes } }
		}
	}

	id = IMX_soviet_collapse_focus_tree
